# STC Assessor's Manual Volume II (2025) — M And N

> Source: michigan.gov STC Assessors-Manual, retrieved 2026-08-03.
> Original PDF at source-pdfs/assessor-manual/stc-assessors-manual-vol2-11m-and-n.pdf, text via pdftotext -layout.
> COST SCHEDULES. These are tables. Extraction preserves most row labels and cost
> figures, but ⚠ ALWAYS VERIFY A COST FIGURE AGAINST THE PDF before using it in a
> valuation — a mis-parsed table cell is indistinguishable from a correct one in text.
> Volume I = residential and agricultural. Volume II = commercial and industrial.

                                                             AVERAGE CLASS D POLE

miscellaneous school ground equipment.The masonry buildings have
brick or block with heavy roof framing and windows on the better                    NOT INCLUDED IN COSTS: Elevators, sprinklers, lockers, therapy
qualities.                                                                          or recreational equipment.
The frame structures are constructed with either wood or steel fram-
ed walls. The exterior covers are typically stucco, wood siding or met-
al siding. Floors at the lower qualities may be unfinished while the
better qualities have sealed floors and walls with some finished shop
area and cabinets.

OCCUPANCY DESCRIPTION: These structures are three stories or                         INCLUDED IN COSTS: Architects’ fees and contractors’ overhead and
fewer where each studio, one or two bedroom suite has limited indi-                  profit. Intercoms and master antenna/cable services as indicated.

eling, good detailing in molding and trim and high cost floor finishes.              furnishings, and kitchen, laundry, therapy or recreation equipment.

The structures commonly are solid masonry or wood frame walls.
Some of the special refinements found include task lighting, railings,
intercoms, television jacks and antennas, and emergency call systems.
